<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-191-4-d" aria-label="Link to rule 191.4.d">191.4.d.</a> That player may make decisions about any game effects created from "When you play me" effects of Permanents.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-191-5" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-191-5" aria-label="Link to rule 191.5">191.5.</a> For Abilities, they are the Ability’s Controller.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-191-5-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-191-5-a" aria-label="Link to rule 191.5.a">191.5.a.</a> By default, the Controller of an Ability’s Source is the Controller of that Ability.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-191-5-a-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-191-5-a-1" aria-label="Link to rule 191.5.a.1">191.5.a.1.</a> If an Ability’s Source is located in a non-Board Zone, the Ability’s Controller is the Owner of the Source.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-191-5-a-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-191-5-a-2" aria-label="Link to rule 191.5.a.2">191.5.a.2.</a> If an Ability allows players other than the Controller or Owner of its Source to play it, the player that plays the Ability Controls the Ability.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-191-5-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-191-5-b" aria-label="Link to rule 191.5.b">191.5.b.</a> Changes to Control of an Ability’s Source do not change Control of that Ability.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-191-5-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-191-5-c" aria-label="Link to rule 191.5.c">191.5.c.</a> That player chooses targets.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-191-5-d" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-191-5-d" aria-label="Link to rule 191.5.d">191.5.d.</a> That player chooses modes.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-191-5-e" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-191-5-e" aria-label="Link to rule 191.5.e">191.5.e.</a> That player pays costs.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-192" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-192" aria-label="Link to rule 192">192.</a> When a game effect or rules text refers to the Controller of a specific object, it can be referring to either context interchangeably.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-192-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-192-1" aria-label="Link to rule 192.1">192.1.</a> The method of assignment of control is different, but the status of Control is the same across all Game Objects.</p>
</div>

### 193. Winning {#rule-193}


<div id="rule-194" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-194" aria-label="Link to rule 194">194.</a> Players win Riftbound games primarily through gaining points.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-194-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-194-1" aria-label="Link to rule 194.1">194.1.</a> Players can gain points a number of ways:</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-194-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-194-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 194.1.a">194.1.a.</a> Holding a Battlefield.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-469">469</a>. Scoring for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-194-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-194-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 194.1.b">194.1.b.</a> Conquering a Battlefield.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-469">469</a>. Scoring for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-194-1-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-194-1-c" aria-label="Link to rule 194.1.c">194.1.c.</a> Spells, Triggered Abilities and Activated Abilities that instruct them to gain one or more points.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-194-1-d" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-194-1-d" aria-label="Link to rule 194.1.d">194.1.d.</a> When an opponent Burns Out and picks that player to gain 1 point.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-432">432</a>. Burn Out for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-194-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-194-2" aria-label="Link to rule 194.2">194.2.</a> A player wins the game if, in a cleanup, they have points greater than or equal to the Victory Score and more points than any other player.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-318">318</a>. Cleanups for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-194-2-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-194-2-a" aria-label="Link to rule 194.2.a">194.2.a.</a> If more than one player has points greater than or equal to the Victory Score, whichever player has more points wins.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-194-2-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-194-2-b" aria-label="Link to rule 194.2.b">194.2.b.</a> If those players have the same number of points, play continues until one player has more points in a cleanup.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-194-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-194-3" aria-label="Link to rule 194.3">194.3.</a> The Victory Score is 8 points by default.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-194-3-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-194-3-a" aria-label="Link to rule 194.3.a">194.3.a.</a> Some game modes or card effects may alter the Victory Score.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-483">483</a>. Modes of Play for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-194-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-194-4" aria-label="Link to rule 194.4">194.4.</a> Players cannot have less than 0 points.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-194-4-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-194-4-a" aria-label="Link to rule 194.4.a">194.4.a.</a> If a player would lose 1 or more points while they have 0 points, nothing occurs.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-194-4-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-194-4-b" aria-label="Link to rule 194.4.b">194.4.b.</a> Any effects that trigger on a player losing points do not trigger.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-195" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-195" aria-label="Link to rule 195">195.</a> A player also wins the game if an effect instructs them to do so, or if they are the only player remaining in the game.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-195-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-195-1" aria-label="Link to rule 195.1">195.1.</a> If an effect instructs a player to lose the game, that player is immediately removed from the game.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-649">649</a>. Conceding for more information about being removed from the game.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-196" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-196" aria-label="Link to rule 196">196.</a> When a player wins the game, the game ends.</p>
</div>

### 197. Locations {#rule-197}


<div id="rule-198" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-198" aria-label="Link to rule 198">198.</a> Locations are the Game Objects and Zones where permanents and runes may be located.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-198-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-198-1" aria-label="Link to rule 198.1">198.1.</a> Locations include the Battlefields and the Bases.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-198-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-198-2" aria-label="Link to rule 198.2">198.2.</a> A permanent’s Location is a characteristic of that permanent that may be referenced by Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-199" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-199" aria-label="Link to rule 199">199.</a> A Game Object that comprises a Location is also a Game Object at that location.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-199-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-199-1" aria-label="Link to rule 199.1">199.1.</a> A Battlefield is both a location and also a Game Object at that location.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-200" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-200" aria-label="Link to rule 200">200.</a> Locations can be Destinations and Origins for movement.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-447">447</a>. Movement for more information.</p>
</div>

### 201. Costs {#rule-201}


<div id="rule-202" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-202" aria-label="Link to rule 202">202.</a> Some Game Effects will instruct players to pay resources, spend counters or XP, or perform game actions as a Cost in order to perform a linked Effect.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-203" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-203" aria-label="Link to rule 203">203.</a> Costs must be paid in order to get their linked Effect.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-203-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-203-1" aria-label="Link to rule 203.1">203.1.</a> Paying a Cost requires a player to perform the appropriate game action: pay resources, spend counters or XP, kill units, etc.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-203-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-203-2" aria-label="Link to rule 203.2">203.2.</a> The Cost is still considered paid even if the associated game action is replaced.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-203-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-203-3" aria-label="Link to rule 203.3">203.3.</a> If the game action associated with a Cost is impossible for any reason such that a player cannot perform it, then they cannot pay the Cost and they will not execute the linked Effect.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-204" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-204" aria-label="Link to rule 204">204.</a> Costs paid in this way might be:</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-204-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-204-1" aria-label="Link to rule 204.1">204.1.</a> Base Costs: These costs are the most fundamental costs that must be paid to finalize a card or ability.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-204-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-204-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 204.1.a">204.1.a.</a> On cards, the Base Cost is found in the upper left corner of the card.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-204-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-204-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 204.1.b">204.1.b.</a> On activated abilities, the Base Cost is the resource or instruction written before the “:”. If there are multiple choices for costs to pay for an activated ability before the “:”, the Base Cost is whatever cost the player chooses from among those options.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: Legion Marauder is a unit that reads in part “[Empower] — [1] or [C].” This is functionally short for “[1] or [C]: Empower me. Play only if not empowered.” When finalizing the ability to the chain, the controller of the chain item will choose one of the base costs to pay. That is the base cost of the ability.</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-204-1-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-204-1-c" aria-label="Link to rule 204.1.c">204.1.c.</a> On Triggered Abilities, the Base Cost if it is present is the Costs within Instructions that appears as the first part of the effect. It must be paid to finalize the triggered ability.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-204-3">204.3</a>. Costs within Instructions for more information.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: Overzealous Fan is a unit that reads “When I defend, you may kill me to move an attacking unit to its base.” In order to finalize the ability to the chain, its controller must kill Overzealous Fan.</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-204-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-204-2" aria-label="Link to rule 204.2">204.2.</a> Additional Costs: These Costs are in addition to the base cost of the card or ability and will include “as an additional cost.”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-204-2-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-204-2-a" aria-label="Link to rule 204.2.a">204.2.a.</a> Additional Costs must be paid to finalize the card or ability, in addition to the base cost.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-204-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-204-3" aria-label="Link to rule 204.3">204.3.</a> Costs within Instructions: These Costs are part of the resolution of a spell or ability, and will take the form “[do X] to [do Y].”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-204-3-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-204-3-a" aria-label="Link to rule 204.3.a">204.3.a.</a> When Costs within Instructions appear in spells, activated abilities, or in later parts of triggered abilities, the cost must be paid to get the effect during resolution of the spell or ability.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: Bullet Time is a spell that reads “Pay any amount of [A] to deal that much damage to all enemy units at a battlefield.” As the spell resolves, its controller pays any amount of [A] as a cost, and deals that much damage to all units at a battlefield as the effect.</p></blockquote>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: Ancient Henge is a gear that reads “[Reaction][&gt;] [E]: Pay any amount of Energy to Add that much [A].” As the ability resolves, its controller pays any amount of Energy as a cost, and Adds that much [A] as the effect.</p></blockquote>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: Insightful Investigator is a unit that reads “When you play me, choose an opponent. They reveal their hand. You may pay 2 XP to choose a card from their hand. If you do, they discard that card and draw 1.” As the ability resolves, its controller may pay 2 XP as a cost, and chooses a card from that player’s hand as the effect.</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-204-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-204-4" aria-label="Link to rule 204.4">204.4.</a> Applied Costs: These Costs are applied to one or more Game Actions, and typically take the form of a passive ability with a Cost within Instructions preceded by “must.”</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: Mageseeker Investigator reads in part “Opponents must pay [A] for each unit beyond the first to move multiple units to my battlefield at the same time.” This is an applied cost.</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-204-4-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-204-4-a" aria-label="Link to rule 204.4.a">204.4.a.</a> The associated Game Action is the linked Effect for the Cost.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-204-4-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-204-4-b" aria-label="Link to rule 204.4.b">204.4.b.</a> Applied Costs are paid as the Game Action is performed. They do not use the chain and cannot be reacted to.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-204-4-b-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-204-4-b-1" aria-label="Link to rule 204.4.b.1">204.4.b.1.</a> Abilities that Add resources with the Reaction keyword can be activated at any time when costs need to be paid.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-430">430</a>. Add for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-204-4-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-204-4-c" aria-label="Link to rule 204.4.c">204.4.c.</a> If a player can’t pay or chooses not to pay the Applied Cost, they cannot perform the associated Game Action.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-205" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-205" aria-label="Link to rule 205">205.</a> An instruction that requires a player to pay resources or spend counters or XP that does not also have a linked Effect, is not a Cost.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: A card effect reads, “When I attack, you may pay [4][C]. If you do, kill a unit here.” Paying [4][C] in this way is not a cost of the ability, base or otherwise, but a game action being performed by a player. The later instruction checks whether the game action was performed, not whether a cost was paid—if paying [4][C] was replaced for any reason, the later linked instruction will not execute.</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-206" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-206" aria-label="Link to rule 206">206.</a> Effects that need to determine a card or token’s cost for any purpose always use its printed—or if a copy effect is applied, copied—cost, even if that cost is increased, decreased, or ignored as the card is played.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: Sky Splitter is a spell that costs [8] and says in part “This spell's Energy cost is reduced by the highest Might among units you control.” Lux, Illuminated is a unit that says “When you play a spell that costs 5 or more, give me +3 [M] this turn.” If a player who controls Lux plays Sky Splitter, Lux’s ability will trigger, regardless of how much was actually paid for Sky Splitter.</p></blockquote>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: Defy is a spell which reads “Counter a spell that costs no more than [4] and no more than [A].” Rocket Barrage is a spell that costs [4][C] and has a Repeat cost of [4][C]. Rocket Barrage is a legal target for Defy even if Rocket Barrage’s Repeat cost is paid, because Defy only checks the printed or copied cost of its target.</p></blockquote>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: Atakhan is a unit that reads in part “You may kill a friendly unit as an additional cost to play me. If you do, I cost [1] less for each Energy it costs and [Y] less for each Power it costs.” If a player plays Atakhan and chooses to kill a Reflection token that is currently a copy of Noxus Hopeful, Atakhan will cost [4] less.</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-206-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-206-1" aria-label="Link to rule 206.1">206.1.</a> Effects that need to determine an ability’s cost for any purpose use its base cost, ignoring any alterations to that base cost unless otherwise specified.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: A card reads “When you play an activated ability with Energy cost [7] or more, you may exhaust me to ready up to 2 runes.” A gear has an activated ability that reads “[12], Banish this: Choose an opponent. Banish all units they control. This ability costs [1] less for each point the chosen opponent has.” The ability of the first card will trigger when the activated ability is played, even if the chosen opponent has 6 or more points.</p></blockquote>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: An equipment has an ability that reads “Equip [4][A]. This ability costs [1] less for each occupied enemy battlefield.” If its controller plays a unit with Weaponmaster, the Weaponmaster trigger will refer to the cost including the alterations. This is because Weaponmaster specifies that it includes modifications to the cost.</p></blockquote>
</div>

## 300. Playing the Game {#rule-300}


### 301. The Turn {#rule-301}


<div id="rule-302" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-302" aria-label="Link to rule 302">302.</a> Play continues cyclically until one player wins.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-303" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-303" aria-label="Link to rule 303">303.</a> The phases of a turn are rigid, but the actions taken during those steps can be done in any order, unless otherwise specified.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-303-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-303-1" aria-label="Link to rule 303.1">303.1.</a> Game Actions of any nature are performed one at a time and are executed completely.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-303-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-303-2" aria-label="Link to rule 303.2">303.2.</a> Game Actions cannot be performed simultaneously for any reason.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-303-2-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-303-2-a" aria-label="Link to rule 303.2.a">303.2.a.</a> If one or more game actions, game effects, or Triggered Abilities are activated simultaneously, then Turn Order is referenced to organize the sequence of actions, starting with the current Turn Player.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-382">382</a>. Triggered Abilities for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-304" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-304" aria-label="Link to rule 304">304.</a> The Turn Player is the player taking the current turn.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-305" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-305" aria-label="Link to rule 305">305.</a> When there are no items on the Chain and the Turn Player cannot or chooses not to perform any Discretionary Actions, the current phase or step of the turn ends and the next phase, step, or turn begins.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-306" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-306" aria-label="Link to rule 306">306.</a> The Turn Player changes when the current Turn Player reaches the End of all of the Phases of their Turn.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-307" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-307" aria-label="Link to rule 307">307.</a> States of the Turn</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-308" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-308" aria-label="Link to rule 308">308.</a> At any given time, the turn is in either a Neutral State or a Showdown State.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-308-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-308-1" aria-label="Link to rule 308.1">308.1.</a> If a Showdown or Combat is in progress, the turn is in a Showdown State.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-308-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-308-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 308.1.a">308.1.a.</a> Only cards and abilities with the Action or Reaction keywords can be played or activated in a Showdown State.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-308-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-308-2" aria-label="Link to rule 308.2">308.2.</a> If no Showdown or Combat is in progress, the turn is in a Neutral State.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-309" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-309" aria-label="Link to rule 309">309.</a> At any given time, the turn is in either an Open State or a Closed State.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-309-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-309-1" aria-label="Link to rule 309.1">309.1.</a> If a Chain exists, the turn is in a Closed State.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-309-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-309-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 309.1.a">309.1.a.</a> Only cards and abilities with the Reaction keyword can be played or activated in a Closed State.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-309-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-309-2" aria-label="Link to rule 309.2">309.2.</a> If no Chain exists, the turn is in an Open State.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-310" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-310" aria-label="Link to rule 310">310.</a> These descriptions can be combined, such that the turn is always in one of these four states:</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-310-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-310-1" aria-label="Link to rule 310.1">310.1.</a> Neutral Open: There is no Showdown or Combat in progress and no Chain exists.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-310-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-310-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 310.1.a">310.1.a.</a> By default, cards can be played and abilities activated only when a player has priority on their turn in a Neutral Open state.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-310-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-310-2" aria-label="Link to rule 310.2">310.2.</a> Neutral Closed: There is no Showdown or Combat in progress and a Chain exists.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-310-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-310-3" aria-label="Link to rule 310.3">310.3.</a> Showdown Open: A Showdown or Combat is in progress and no Chain exists.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-310-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-310-4" aria-label="Link to rule 310.4">310.4.</a> Showdown Closed: A Showdown or Combat is in progress and a Chain exists.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-311" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-311" aria-label="Link to rule 311">311.</a> Priority and Focus</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-312" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-312" aria-label="Link to rule 312">312.</a> At any given time, up to one player has Priority.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-312-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-312-1" aria-label="Link to rule 312.1">312.1.</a> Priority is the singular exclusive right to take Discretionary Actions.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-411-1">411.1</a>. Discretionary Actions for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-312-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-312-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 312.1.a">312.1.a.</a> The player with Priority can take appropriately timed Discretionary Actions.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-312-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-312-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 312.1.b">312.1.b.</a> If no player has Priority, no player can take Discretionary Actions.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-312-1-b-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-312-1-b-1" aria-label="Link to rule 312.1.b.1">312.1.b.1.</a> Players can always take and make choices for Limited Actions when instructed, regardless of Priority.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-312-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-312-2" aria-label="Link to rule 312.2">312.2.</a> A player receives Priority at the following times:</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-312-2-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-312-2-a" aria-label="Link to rule 312.2.a">312.2.a.</a> When the turn is in a Neutral Open State during their Main Phase.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-312-2-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-312-2-b" aria-label="Link to rule 312.2.b">312.2.b.</a> When the turn is in a Showdown State and they gain Focus.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-312-2-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-312-2-c" aria-label="Link to rule 312.2.c">312.2.c.</a> When the turn is in a Closed State, all pending chain items finish being finalized, and they control the next item on the Chain.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-312-2-d" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-312-2-d" aria-label="Link to rule 312.2.d">312.2.d.</a> When the turn is in a Closed State, they are the next Player in Turn Order, and the player with Priority passes.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-312-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-312-3" aria-label="Link to rule 312.3">312.3.</a> When a player is granted Priority, it is either created if no player has it or taken from the player with Priority.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-313" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-313" aria-label="Link to rule 313">313.</a> At any given time, up to one player has Focus.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-313-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-313-1" aria-label="Link to rule 313.1">313.1.</a> Focus is the permission to take appropriately timed Discretionary Actions when the turn is in a Showdown Open State.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-307">307</a>. States of the Turn for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-313-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-313-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 313.1.a">313.1.a.</a> The player with Focus must obey any additional restrictions on which Discretionary Actions may be performed.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: A player with Focus may not play spells or activate abilities that don't have the Action or Reaction keywords.</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-313-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-313-2" aria-label="Link to rule 313.2">313.2.</a> A player who gains Focus also gains Priority.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-313-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-313-3" aria-label="Link to rule 313.3">313.3.</a> A player who passes Priority retains Focus.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-313-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-313-4" aria-label="Link to rule 313.4">313.4.</a> A player may not make discretionary actions with Focus unless they also possess Priority.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-313-5" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-313-5" aria-label="Link to rule 313.5">313.5.</a> If the turn is in a Neutral State, no player has Focus.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-314" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-314" aria-label="Link to rule 314">314.</a> Phases of the Turn</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-315" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-315" aria-label="Link to rule 315">315.</a> Start of Turn</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-315-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-315-1" aria-label="Link to rule 315.1">315.1.</a> Awaken Phase</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-315-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-315-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 315.1.a">315.1.a.</a> The following Task becomes Outstanding:</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-315-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-315-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 315.1.b">315.1.b.</a> 1. The Turn Player readies all Game Objects they control that are able to be readied.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-416">416</a>. Ready for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-315-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-315-2" aria-label="Link to rule 315.2">315.2.</a> Beginning Phase</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-315-2-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-315-2-a" aria-label="Link to rule 315.2.a">315.2.a.</a> Beginning Step</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-315-2-a-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-315-2-a-1" aria-label="Link to rule 315.2.a.1">315.2.a.1.</a> At the start of Beginning Phase game effects take place.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-315-2-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-315-2-b" aria-label="Link to rule 315.2.b">315.2.b.</a> Scoring Step</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-315-2-b-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-315-2-b-1" aria-label="Link to rule 315.2.b.1">315.2.b.1.</a> The following Task becomes Outstanding:</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-315-2-b-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-315-2-b-2" aria-label="Link to rule 315.2.b.2">315.2.b.2.</a> 1. The Turn Player Holds all Battlefields they Control.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-469">469</a>. Scoring for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-315-2-b-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-315-2-b-3" aria-label="Link to rule 315.2.b.3">315.2.b.3.</a> Reminder: In Modes of Play with Teams, Battlefields controlled by a Teammate of the Turn Player during this step are disqualified from being scored this turn by that team.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-315-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-315-3" aria-label="Link to rule 315.3">315.3.</a> Channel Phase</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-315-3-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-315-3-a" aria-label="Link to rule 315.3.a">315.3.a.</a> The following Task becomes Outstanding:</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-315-3-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-315-3-b" aria-label="Link to rule 315.3.b">315.3.b.</a> 1. The Turn Player channels 2 runes from their Rune Deck.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-431">431</a>. Channel for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-315-3-b-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-315-3-b-1" aria-label="Link to rule 315.3.b.1">315.3.b.1.</a> If there are fewer than 2 runes in the Rune Deck, they channel as many as possible.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-315-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-315-4" aria-label="Link to rule 315.4">315.4.</a> Draw Phase</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-315-4-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-315-4-a" aria-label="Link to rule 315.4.a">315.4.a.</a> The following Task becomes Outstanding:</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-315-4-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-315-4-b" aria-label="Link to rule 315.4.b">315.4.b.</a> 1. The Turn Player draws 1.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-315-4-b-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-315-4-b-1" aria-label="Link to rule 315.4.b.1">315.4.b.1.</a> If there are no cards remaining in their Main Deck to draw, the Turn Player has been Burned Out.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-432">432</a>. Burn Out for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-315-4-b-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-315-4-b-2" aria-label="Link to rule 315.4.b.2">315.4.b.2.</a> After completing the Burn Out the Turn Player still Draws 1.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316" aria-label="Link to rule 316">316.</a> Main Phase</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-1" aria-label="Link to rule 316.1">316.1.</a> When all steps of the Start of Turn have been completed, the Main Phase begins.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-2" aria-label="Link to rule 316.2">316.2.</a> The following Tasks become Outstanding in the specified order:</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-3" aria-label="Link to rule 316.3">316.3.</a> 1. Each player's Rune Pool empties. Any unspent Energy and Power are lost.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-165">165</a>. Rune Pools for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-4" aria-label="Link to rule 316.4">316.4.</a> 2. At the start of Main Phase game effects take place.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-5" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-5" aria-label="Link to rule 316.5">316.5.</a> The Main Phase has no defined structure.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-5-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-5-a" aria-label="Link to rule 316.5.a">316.5.a.</a> A player may take any number of Discretionary Actions they are able to perform during this phase.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-411-1">411.1</a>. Discretionary Actions for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-5-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-5-b" aria-label="Link to rule 316.5.b">316.5.b.</a> This is denoted as a Neutral Open State, and only the Turn Player has the ability to play spells or activate abilities.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-307">307</a>. States of the Turn for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-5-b-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-5-b-1" aria-label="Link to rule 316.5.b.1">316.5.b.1.</a> In Modes of Play with teammates, the Turn Player's teammates may play spells and activate abilities, including ones without Action or Reaction. They can only do so when the Turn Player invites them to do so with their own Priority.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-6" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-6" aria-label="Link to rule 316.6">316.6.</a> As a result of a player taking Discretionary Actions, one or more structured phases may occur.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-7" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-7" aria-label="Link to rule 316.7">316.7.</a> Combat</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-7-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-7-a" aria-label="Link to rule 316.7.a">316.7.a.</a> A Combat occurs as a result of Units controlled by opposing players being present at the same Battlefield.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-7-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-7-b" aria-label="Link to rule 316.7.b">316.7.b.</a> This could be the result of a Standard Move Intrinsic Ability, a Spell, or other Game Effect.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-7-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-7-c" aria-label="Link to rule 316.7.c">316.7.c.</a> The source effect does not change the structure or flow of Combat once initiated.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-7-d" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-7-d" aria-label="Link to rule 316.7.d">316.7.d.</a> A Combat can only occur between two players.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-461">461</a>. Combat for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-7-e" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-7-e" aria-label="Link to rule 316.7.e">316.7.e.</a> Play proceeds following the steps of combat.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-465">465</a>. The Steps of Combat for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-7-f" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-7-f" aria-label="Link to rule 316.7.f">316.7.f.</a> Combat will also include a Showdown.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-8" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-8" aria-label="Link to rule 316.8">316.8.</a> Showdowns</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-8-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-8-a" aria-label="Link to rule 316.8.a">316.8.a.</a> A Showdown occurs when a Combat occurs.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-8-a-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-8-a-1" aria-label="Link to rule 316.8.a.1">316.8.a.1.</a> Showdowns that occur as a result of Combat are a Sub-Phase of Combat.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-8-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-8-b" aria-label="Link to rule 316.8.b">316.8.b.</a> A Showdown is marked as Staged at a Battlefield in the cleanup after the Contested status is applied to that Battlefield.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-8-b-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-8-b-1" aria-label="Link to rule 316.8.b.1">316.8.b.1.</a> Showdowns that occur as a result of a player moving to an empty Battlefield are a stand-alone Phase and do not create a Combat.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-8-b-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 4">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-8-b-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 316.8.b.1.a">316.8.b.1.a.</a> These Showdowns are called Non-Combat Showdowns. During the proceedings of a Non-Combat Showdown, units controlled by a different player may become present at the Battlefield where the Showdown is ongoing. This will cause the Showdown to become a Combat Showdown in the following cleanup.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-8-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-8-c" aria-label="Link to rule 316.8.c">316.8.c.</a> A Showdown is a structured Window of Opportunity where Players may play cards and activate abilities with Action or Reaction.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-341">341</a>. Showdowns for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-9" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-9" aria-label="Link to rule 316.9">316.9.</a> When a player has no more Discretionary Actions they wish to execute, they must indicate they are ending their turn.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-9-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-9-a" aria-label="Link to rule 316.9.a">316.9.a.</a> This ends the Main Phase.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-316-9-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-316-9-b" aria-label="Link to rule 316.9.b">316.9.b.</a> Play proceeds to the Ending Phase.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-317" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-317" aria-label="Link to rule 317">317.</a> Ending Phase</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-317-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-317-1" aria-label="Link to rule 317.1">317.1.</a> Ending Step</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-317-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-317-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 317.1.a">317.1.a.</a> At the end of this turn and at the end of turn Game Effects take place.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-317-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-317-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 317.1.b">317.1.b.</a> Specific game effects and abilities will reference this timing and phase as necessary.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-317-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-317-2" aria-label="Link to rule 317.2">317.2.</a> Expiration Step</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-317-2-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-317-2-a" aria-label="Link to rule 317.2.a">317.2.a.</a> Invoke an Ending Special Cleanup.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-318">318</a>. Cleanups for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-317-2-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-317-2-b" aria-label="Link to rule 317.2.b">317.2.b.</a> Insert “3c. Heal all Units.”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-317-2-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-317-2-c" aria-label="Link to rule 317.2.c">317.2.c.</a> Insert “3d. All ‘this turn’ effects expire simultaneously.”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-317-2-d" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-317-2-d" aria-label="Link to rule 317.2.d">317.2.d.</a> Insert “3e. Each player’s Rune Pool empties. Any unspent Energy and Power are lost.”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-317-2-e" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-317-2-e" aria-label="Link to rule 317.2.e">317.2.e.</a> The following Task becomes Outstanding:</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-317-2-f" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-317-2-f" aria-label="Link to rule 317.2.f">317.2.f.</a> 1. If any items underwent the FEPR process, return to the start of the Expiration Step</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-317-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-317-3" aria-label="Link to rule 317.3">317.3.</a> The next player with their Turn queued becomes the Turn Player.</p>
</div>

### 318. Cleanups {#rule-318}


<div id="rule-319" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-319" aria-label="Link to rule 319">319.</a> A Cleanup will be made an Outstanding Task at the following times:</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-319-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-319-1" aria-label="Link to rule 319.1">319.1.</a> After the game transitions to or from an Open or Closed state</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-319-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-319-2" aria-label="Link to rule 319.2">319.2.</a> After the game transitions between Phases, unless specified otherwise</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-319-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-319-3" aria-label="Link to rule 319.3">319.3.</a> After a Pending Item is added to the Chain</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-319-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-319-4" aria-label="Link to rule 319.4">319.4.</a> After a Pending Item becomes a Finalized Item on the Chain</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-319-5" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-319-5" aria-label="Link to rule 319.5">319.5.</a> After a Chain Item is removed from the Chain for any reason</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-319-6" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-319-6" aria-label="Link to rule 319.6">319.6.</a> After any number of Game Objects enter or leave the Board</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-319-7" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-319-7" aria-label="Link to rule 319.7">319.7.</a> After the status of any number of Game Objects changes for any reason</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-319-8" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-319-8" aria-label="Link to rule 319.8">319.8.</a> After a Move is completed</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-320" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-320" aria-label="Link to rule 320">320.</a> While a Cleanup is occurring, Chain Items cannot be Finalized or Resolved.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-320-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-320-1" aria-label="Link to rule 320.1">320.1.</a> New Pending Items can be added, but Finalized Items cannot be executed and Priority and Focus are not passed or awarded.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-321" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-321" aria-label="Link to rule 321">321.</a> Similarly, while Chain Items are Resolving, a Cleanup cannot occur.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-321-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-321-1" aria-label="Link to rule 321.1">321.1.</a> If an event occurs during the Resolution of a Chain Item that qualifies for a Cleanup, that Cleanup will be made an Outstanding Task.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-322" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-322" aria-label="Link to rule 322">322.</a> If an event occurs during a Cleanup that qualifies for a Cleanup, another Cleanup will occur immediately after the first completes, repeating until a Cleanup occurs with no new change in the game’s state.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-322-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-322-1" aria-label="Link to rule 322.1">322.1.</a> These new Cleanups are themselves Outstanding Tasks.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-323" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-323" aria-label="Link to rule 323">323.</a> When a Cleanup occurs, the following Tasks become Outstanding in the order described:</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-323-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-323-1" aria-label="Link to rule 323.1">323.1.</a> 1. If a player has points greater than or equal to the Victory Score, and more points than every opponent, that player wins.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-323-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-323-2" aria-label="Link to rule 323.2">323.2.</a> 2. Assign or Remove the Attacker or Defender designation from Units as needed if there is a Combat in progress</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-323-2-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-323-2-a" aria-label="Link to rule 323.2.a">323.2.a.</a> If there are Units present at the Battlefield the Combat is taking place at, but do not have a designation, they gain the same designation as their Controller now</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-323-2-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-323-2-b" aria-label="Link to rule 323.2.b">323.2.b.</a> If there are Units present at the Battlefield the Combat is taking place at, but have the opposite designation of their controller, they lose that designation, and gain the same designation as their controller now</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-323-2-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-323-2-c" aria-label="Link to rule 323.2.c">323.2.c.</a> If there are Units at locations other than the Battlefield that the Combat is taking place at, but have either Attacker or Defender designations, they lose those designations now</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-323-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-323-3" aria-label="Link to rule 323.3">323.3.</a> 3. Handle outstanding board state</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-323-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-323-4" aria-label="Link to rule 323.4">323.4.</a> 3a. All Units that have Lethal Damage marked on them are killed and placed in their owners' Trash.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-142-4">142.4</a>. For more information on lethal damage.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-323-5" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-323-5" aria-label="Link to rule 323.5">323.5.</a> 4. Players lose control of any controlled Battlefields without their Units occupying them if the turn is in an Open State and there is no Showdown or Combat ongoing there.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-323-6" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-323-6" aria-label="Link to rule 323.6">323.6.</a> 5. Recall all of the following Game Objects, unless an effect would indicate otherwise: Unattached non-Unit Gear located at Battlefields Non-Unit Runes located at Battlefields Unattached Permanents and Runes in Bases other than their controller’s</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-323-6-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-323-6-a" aria-label="Link to rule 323.6.a">323.6.a.</a> Remove all Hidden cards from all Battlefields that are not controlled by the same player and place them in their owner's Trash.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-323-7" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-323-7" aria-label="Link to rule 323.7">323.7.</a> 6. Mark a Showdown as Staged at each Battlefield that Contested was applied to and that has no ongoing Combat.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-323-7-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-323-7-a" aria-label="Link to rule 323.7.a">323.7.a.</a> The Showdown remains Staged at that Battlefield as long as it is Contested and has units present controlled by the player that applied Contested.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-323-8" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-323-8" aria-label="Link to rule 323.8">323.8.</a> 7. Mark a Combat as Staged at each Battlefield that Contested was applied to that have Units present controlled by opposing players, and that has no ongoing Combat.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-323-9" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-323-9" aria-label="Link to rule 323.9">323.9.</a> 7a. If Units of two opposing players are no longer present at a Battlefield that has a Combat Staged before it has opened, the Combat will cease being Staged</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-323-10" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-323-10" aria-label="Link to rule 323.10">323.10.</a> 8. Remove Contested status from each Battlefield without Units controlled by the player who applied Contested to that Battlefield and without a Showdown or Combat ongoing there.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-323-10-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-323-10-a" aria-label="Link to rule 323.10.a">323.10.a.</a> If as a result of the removal of Contested status there are Units located at an uncontested Battlefield that their controller does not control, their controller applies Contested status to that Battlefield.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-323-11" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-323-11" aria-label="Link to rule 323.11">323.11.</a> 9. If the current state is a Neutral Open State and one or more Showdowns are Staged at Battlefields without a Combat staged, the Turn Player chooses one of those Battlefields. A Showdown begins there.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-323-12" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-323-12" aria-label="Link to rule 323.12">323.12.</a> 10. If the current state is a Neutral Open State and one or more Combats are Staged at Battlefields, the Turn Player chooses one of those Battlefields. Combat begins there.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-323-13" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-323-13" aria-label="Link to rule 323.13">323.13.</a> 10a. If the current state is Showdown Open State and Combat is Staged at a Battlefield where there is a Non-Combat Showdown ongoing, that Showdown becomes a Combat Showdown.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-324" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-324" aria-label="Link to rule 324">324.</a> Special Cleanups are Cleanup steps invoked at specific times that have additional steps not present in a normal Cleanup.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-324-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-324-1" aria-label="Link to rule 324.1">324.1.</a> When a Special Cleanup is invoked, the unique steps added will be inserted and defined by the sub- section that invokes it.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: When a Combat Cleanup is invoked, the Combat section defines what steps are added to the Cleanup.</p></blockquote>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-468">468</a>. The Resolution Step for more information.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: When an End of Turn Cleanup is invoked, the End of Turn Phase subsection defines what steps are added to the Cleanup.</p></blockquote>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-317">317</a>. The Ending Phase for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-324-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-324-2" aria-label="Link to rule 324.2">324.2.</a> If events during a Special Cleanup require another Cleanup, a normal Cleanup is invoked, not another iteration of the Special Cleanup.</p>
</div>

### 325. Chains and Showdowns {#rule-325}


<div id="rule-326" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-326" aria-label="Link to rule 326">326.</a> Players can act during the following Windows of Opportunity that occur during the course of regular play:</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-326-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-326-1" aria-label="Link to rule 326.1">326.1.</a> During a Chain</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-326-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-326-2" aria-label="Link to rule 326.2">326.2.</a> During a Showdown</p>
</div>

### 327. Chains {#rule-327}


<div id="rule-328" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-328" aria-label="Link to rule 328">328.</a> The Chain is a Non-Board Zone that temporarily exists whenever a card or ability is played.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-328-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-328-1" aria-label="Link to rule 328.1">328.1.</a> Cards, abilities, and tokens are placed here as part of the process of being played.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-349">349</a>. The Process of Play for more information.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-360">360</a>. Abilities for more information on Abilities.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-329" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-329" aria-label="Link to rule 329">329.</a> Cards, tokens, and abilities added to the chain are added as Pending Chain Items that become Finalized Chain Items.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-329-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-329-1" aria-label="Link to rule 329.1">329.1.</a> Pending Items are on the Chain.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-329-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-329-2" aria-label="Link to rule 329.2">329.2.</a> Chain Items are Pending until the “Check Legality” step of playing a card.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-349">349</a>. Playing Cards for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-329-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-329-3" aria-label="Link to rule 329.3">329.3.</a> When a Pending Chain Item is no longer Pending it is finalized and becomes a Finalized Chain Item.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-330" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-330" aria-label="Link to rule 330">330.</a> The Chain exists as long as a Chain Item is on it.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-330-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-330-1" aria-label="Link to rule 330.1">330.1.</a> Only one Chain can exist at a time.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-330-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-330-2" aria-label="Link to rule 330.2">330.2.</a> If a card, token, or ability would begin to be played while a Chain already exists, it is placed on the existing Chain.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-331" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-331" aria-label="Link to rule 331">331.</a> The State of the Turn is partially determined by whether or not the Chain currently exists.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-331-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-331-1" aria-label="Link to rule 331.1">331.1.</a> The turn is said to be in a Closed State if a Chain exists.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-331-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-331-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 331.1.a">331.1.a.</a> Cards of all Categories, by default, cannot be played during a Closed State.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-331-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-331-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 331.1.b">331.1.b.</a> Card abilities, by default, cannot be played during a Closed State.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-331-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-331-2" aria-label="Link to rule 331.2">331.2.</a> The turn is said to be in an Open State if no Chain exists.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-332" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-332" aria-label="Link to rule 332">332.</a> Handling Tasks and Resolving Chain Items</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-333" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-333" aria-label="Link to rule 333">333.</a> A Task is one or more steps or processes that one or more Players must perform before continuing with any other actions.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-333-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-333-1" aria-label="Link to rule 333.1">333.1.</a> Tasks include, but are not limited to: Cleanups, the actions performed during the Start of Turn Process, throughout Combat in its various steps, and the actions performed during the End of Turn Process.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-318">318</a>. Cleanups for more information on Cleanups</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-315">315</a>. Start of Turn for more information on the Start of Turn process</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-461">461</a>. Combat for more information on the steps of Combat</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-317">317</a>. Ending Phase for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-334" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-334" aria-label="Link to rule 334">334.</a> Whenever a Player takes one or more actions that incur Tasks they should refer to the process of HOT FEPR: Handle Outstanding Tasks; then Finalize, Execute, Pass, Resolve.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-334-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-334-1" aria-label="Link to rule 334.1">334.1.</a> In the course of Handling Outstanding Tasks, Chain Items may be added to the Chain. They will remain there until the Tasks are complete.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-334-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-334-2" aria-label="Link to rule 334.2">334.2.</a> When all Outstanding Tasks are completed, all pending Chain Items will subsequently be processed by the FEPR process.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-334-2-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-334-2-a" aria-label="Link to rule 334.2.a">334.2.a.</a> During the FEPR process, new Tasks may be incurred. Complete the current step of the process and then pause and complete the necessary Tasks before continuing.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-335" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-335" aria-label="Link to rule 335">335.</a> If there are no Outstanding Tasks, no pending Chain Items, no ongoing Showdown or Combat, and it is the Main Phase, the Turn Player receives priority. If there are no Outstanding Tasks, no pending Chain Items, no ongoing Showdown, and it is any other phase of the turn, proceed to the next substep, step, phase, or turn.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-335-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-335-1" aria-label="Link to rule 335.1">335.1.</a> If there are no Outstanding Tasks, no pending Chain Items, and there is an ongoing Showdown, the player with Focus receives priority.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-336" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-336" aria-label="Link to rule 336">336.</a> When there are no outstanding Tasks and there are pending Chain Items on the Chain, players should refer to the FEPR process to proceed.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-336-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-336-1" aria-label="Link to rule 336.1">336.1.</a> In the sequence of resolving FEPR more Chain Items may become Pending Chain Items. These will be processed by the same FEPR process that produced them.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-337" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-337" aria-label="Link to rule 337">337.</a> Step 1: Finalize</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-337-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-337-1" aria-label="Link to rule 337.1">337.1.</a> If there is at least one Chain Item Pending, the controller of the oldest Pending Chain Item must complete the steps of Playing that Pending Item until it is a Finalized Item or leaves the Chain.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-349">349</a>. Playing Cards for more information on finalizing chain items.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-337-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-337-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 337.1.a">337.1.a.</a> Finalizing an item to the chain does not pass Priority.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-337-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-337-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 337.1.b">337.1.b.</a> Chain Items are Finalized in the order they were appended to the Chain.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-337-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-337-2" aria-label="Link to rule 337.2">337.2.</a> If, after finalizing the Chain Item, that item is a Unit, Gear, or an ability that Adds resources, it resolves immediately—Move to Step 4: Resolve.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-349">349</a>. Playing Cards for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-337-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-337-3" aria-label="Link to rule 337.3">337.3.</a> If, after finalizing the Chain Item, there are still Pending Chain Items, return to Step 1. Finalize.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-337-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-337-4" aria-label="Link to rule 337.4">337.4.</a> If, after finalizing the Chain Item, there are no more items on the chain to be Finalized, the controller of the next item on the chain gains Priority. Move to Step 2: Execute.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-338" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-338" aria-label="Link to rule 338">338.</a> Step 2: Execute</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-338-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-338-1" aria-label="Link to rule 338.1">338.1.</a> The player with Priority may do any the following:</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-338-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-338-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 338.1.a">338.1.a.</a> Play a Card or Activated Ability that is legally timed.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-338-1-a-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-338-1-a-1" aria-label="Link to rule 338.1.a.1">338.1.a.1.</a> Cards and Activated Abilities, by default, cannot be played during a Closed State.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-338-1-a-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-338-1-a-2" aria-label="Link to rule 338.1.a.2">338.1.a.2.</a> A Legally Timed Card or Activated Ability would be one with Reaction or one that will have Reaction when played under appropriate circumstances.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-338-1-a-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-338-1-a-3" aria-label="Link to rule 338.1.a.3">338.1.a.3.</a> Other exceptions may be created during regular play.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-338-1-a-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-338-1-a-4" aria-label="Link to rule 338.1.a.4">338.1.a.4.</a> The Card or Activated Ability will be added to the chain as a Pending Item, following the steps of playing a card.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-338-1-a-5" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-338-1-a-5" aria-label="Link to rule 338.1.a.5">338.1.a.5.</a> This can be an additional item to the item that Started the Chain in the case of the first player with Priority after creating the Chain.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-338-1-a-6" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-338-1-a-6" aria-label="Link to rule 338.1.a.6">338.1.a.6.</a> Whether a Card or Activated Ability is legally timed is evaluated during the “Check Legality” step of Playing a Card.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-349">349</a>. Playing Cards for more information.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-399">399</a>. Playing or Activating Abilities for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-338-1-a-7" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-338-1-a-7" aria-label="Link to rule 338.1.a.7">338.1.a.7.</a> Playing a Card or Activated Ability will create one or more Pending Items. Return to Step 1: Finalize.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-338-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-338-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 338.1.b">338.1.b.</a> Pass Priority</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-338-1-b-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-338-1-b-1" aria-label="Link to rule 338.1.b.1">338.1.b.1.</a> The player with Priority passes Priority to the next Player in Turn Order. Proceed to Step 3: Pass.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-339" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-339" aria-label="Link to rule 339">339.</a> Step 3: Pass</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-339-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-339-1" aria-label="Link to rule 339.1">339.1.</a> If all players have passed Priority in sequence without adding any items to the Chain, proceed to Step 4: Resolve.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-339-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-339-2" aria-label="Link to rule 339.2">339.2.</a> Otherwise, the player with Priority passes Priority to the next Player in Turn Order. Return to Step 2: Execute.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-340" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-340" aria-label="Link to rule 340">340.</a> Step 4: Resolve</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-340-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-340-1" aria-label="Link to rule 340.1">340.1.</a> The newest Finalized Chain Item resolves. Execute its game effects in their entirety.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-349">349</a>. Playing Cards for more information on resolving spells.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-399">399</a>. Playing or Activating Abilities for more information on resolving abilities.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-340-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-340-2" aria-label="Link to rule 340.2">340.2.</a> If the Chain is empty, play proceeds in an Open State.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-340-2-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-340-2-a" aria-label="Link to rule 340.2.a">340.2.a.</a> If this occurs during a Showdown and the chain wasn’t initiated by a triggered ability or an ability that Adds resources, focus passes to the next player in turn order.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-340-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-340-3" aria-label="Link to rule 340.3">340.3.</a> If the Chain is not empty and there are one or more Pending Items, return to Step 1: Finalize.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-340-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-340-4" aria-label="Link to rule 340.4">340.4.</a> If the Chain is not empty and there are no Pending Items, the controller of the newest item on the chain gains Priority. Return to Step 2: Execute.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-341" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-341" aria-label="Link to rule 341">341.</a> Showdowns</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-342" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-342" aria-label="Link to rule 342">342.</a> A Showdown is a Window of Opportunity in which Players have an Open State in which they may play Spells in an alternating fashion.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-342-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-342-1" aria-label="Link to rule 342.1">342.1.</a> Each spell played this way creates a Chain as normal.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-343" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-343" aria-label="Link to rule 343">343.</a> The State of the turn is partially determined by whether or not a Showdown or Combat is in progress.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-343-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-343-1" aria-label="Link to rule 343.1">343.1.</a> The turn is said to be in a Showdown State if a Showdown or Combat is in progress.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-343-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-343-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 343.1.a">343.1.a.</a> Cards of all Categories, by default, cannot be played during a Showdown State.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-343-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-343-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 343.1.b">343.1.b.</a> Card abilities, by default, cannot be played during a Showdown State.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-343-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-343-2" aria-label="Link to rule 343.2">343.2.</a> The turn is said to be in a Neutral State if no Showdown or Combat is in progress.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-344" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-344" aria-label="Link to rule 344">344.</a> A Showdown begins when Control of a Battlefield is Contested during a Cleanup and the turn is in a Neutral Open State.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-344-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-344-1" aria-label="Link to rule 344.1">344.1.</a> If Control of a Battlefield is Contested between two players, then a Showdown will be opened as the first step of Combat. If a Showdown is already ongoing at that Battlefield, it will become a Combat Showdown and a Combat will initiate there.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-461">461</a>. Combat for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-344-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-344-2" aria-label="Link to rule 344.2">344.2.</a> If Control of a Battlefield is Contested, there aren’t units controlled by different players there, and the turn is in a Neutral Open State, a Showdown is opened during the next Cleanup.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-318">318</a>. Cleanups for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-345" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-345" aria-label="Link to rule 345">345.</a> As a Showdown begins, the player who applied Contested status to the Battlefield gains Focus.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-346" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-346" aria-label="Link to rule 346">346.</a> When the last item on the chain resolves and the turn returns to an Open State during a Showdown, Focus passes, and the next Player gains both Focus and Priority.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-346-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-346-1" aria-label="Link to rule 346.1">346.1.</a> Focus will not pass in this way if the chain opened as a result of a triggered ability being added to the chain, nor if it opened as a result of an Add ability being added to the chain.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: the Combat Chain opens as a result of triggered abilities being added to the chain, so when the last item on the Combat Chain resolves and the turn returns to an Open State, Focus will not pass.</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-347" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-347" aria-label="Link to rule 347">347.</a> During a Showdown, the player with Focus may do one of the following:</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-347-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-347-1" aria-label="Link to rule 347.1">347.1.</a> Play a Card or Activated Ability that is legally timed.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-347-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-347-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 347.1.a">347.1.a.</a> The card or activated ability will start a Chain as normal.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-347-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-347-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 347.1.b">347.1.b.</a> When that Chain closes, the next Player in Turn Order gains Focus.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-347-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-347-2" aria-label="Link to rule 347.2">347.2.</a> Pass.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-347-2-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
