<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: Smoke and Mirrors is a spell that reads in part “Choose a unit you control and another unit you control at a different location.” If Smoke and Mirrors is played from facedown, the first unit chosen can be chosen at the battlefield Smoke and Mirrors was played from, so it must be. The second unit chosen explicitly restricts targeting in a way that makes this impossible, so it can be chosen from any location.</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-811-1-d-5" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-811-1-d-5" aria-label="Link to rule 811.1.d.5">811.1.d.5.</a> If a hidden spell or a play effect of a hidden permanent causes you to play a unit, you must choose to play that unit at that battlefield.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-811-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-811-2" aria-label="Link to rule 811.2">811.2.</a> Abilities and instructions of hidden cards other than the choices listed above function as normal.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: Stand United is a spell that has Hidden and says “Buff a friendly unit. Buffs give an additional +1 might to friendly units this turn.” If it’s played from facedown, the first part of its ability must choose a friendly unit at the same battlefield, but the second part of its ability affects all friendly units with buffs, no matter where they are.</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-811-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-811-3" aria-label="Link to rule 811.3">811.3.</a> Instead of being hidden, a card with Hidden may be played for its cost as normal, at its normal timing with no restrictions on targeting.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-811-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-811-4" aria-label="Link to rule 811.4">811.4.</a> Multiple instances of Hidden are redundant.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-811-5" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-811-5" aria-label="Link to rule 811.5">811.5.</a> Hidden, and whether or not a card has Hidden, is a characteristic of the card and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-811-5-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-811-5-a" aria-label="Link to rule 811.5.a">811.5.a.</a> This is true as long as the card is not facedown. A facedown card is not a card with Hidden.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-811-6" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-811-6" aria-label="Link to rule 811.6">811.6.</a> A card that is Hidden gains Reaction the turn after it is hidden while facedown or played from facedown, and may be played any time a card with Reaction may be played as a result.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-811-6-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-811-6-a" aria-label="Link to rule 811.6.a">811.6.a.</a> The property is granted to the card in its facedown state, and is not publicly known.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-812" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-812" aria-label="Link to rule 812">812.</a> Legion</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-812-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-812-1" aria-label="Link to rule 812.1">812.1.</a> Legion is a Dependent Keyword.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-812-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-812-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 812.1.a">812.1.a.</a> It is formatted as "[Legion][&gt;] [Text]".</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-812-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-812-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 812.1.b">812.1.b.</a> Starting from the Keyword to the end of the clause, the entire statement is the Legion Ability.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-812-1-b-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-812-1-b-1" aria-label="Link to rule 812.1.b.1">812.1.b.1.</a> Legion is functionally short for “If you have played another card this turn, this card gains [Text].”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-812-1-b-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-812-1-b-2" aria-label="Link to rule 812.1.b.2">812.1.b.2.</a> The [Text] is the Dependent Ability.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-812-1-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-812-1-c" aria-label="Link to rule 812.1.c">812.1.c.</a> As long as a card different than the one with the Legion ability has been Finalized by you on the same turn then the Dependent Ability is Active on the card with Legion.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-812-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-812-2" aria-label="Link to rule 812.2">812.2.</a> All instances of Legion on cards a player controls are satisfied by that player playing a single card.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: One card has three different Legion Abilities. The Legion Text of all three abilities will be active as long as one card has been finalized by the card's controller earlier in the same turn.</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-812-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-812-3" aria-label="Link to rule 812.3">812.3.</a> Legion, and whether or not a card has Legion, is a characteristic of the card and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-813" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-813" aria-label="Link to rule 813">813.</a> Reaction</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-813-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-813-1" aria-label="Link to rule 813.1">813.1.</a> Reaction is a Permissive keyword.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-813-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-813-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 813.1.a">813.1.a.</a> It can be present on Cards, Rune Abilities, Legend Abilities and Permanent Abilities.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-813-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-813-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 813.1.b">813.1.b.</a> Reaction grants the corresponding card or effect all abilities and permissions of Action.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-813-1-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-813-1-c" aria-label="Link to rule 813.1.c">813.1.c.</a> Reaction, additionally, is functionally short for the following:</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-813-1-c-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-813-1-c-1" aria-label="Link to rule 813.1.c.1">813.1.c.1.</a> On Cards: "This can be played during Closed States on any player's turn."</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-813-1-c-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-813-1-c-2" aria-label="Link to rule 813.1.c.2">813.1.c.2.</a> On Activated Abilities: "This can be activated during Closed States on any player's turn."</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-813-1-d" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-813-1-d" aria-label="Link to rule 813.1.d">813.1.d.</a> Reaction is formatted as “[Reaction]” on cards, or “[Reaction][&gt;]” on abilities.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-813-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-813-2" aria-label="Link to rule 813.2">813.2.</a> The corresponding card or effect with this keyword is not restricted to Closed States or Showdowns. This permission is inclusive of all other timings and options available to the ability as written, Action's permissions, or by default.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-813-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-813-3" aria-label="Link to rule 813.3">813.3.</a> Reaction does not alter the function of any instruction of the Card, Rune, or Effect it is on. It is only Permission.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-813-3-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-813-3-a" aria-label="Link to rule 813.3.a">813.3.a.</a> Playing Units with Reaction still has the inherent restrictions of playing Units without Reaction. It can only be played to the controlling player's base or a battlefield they control.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-813-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-813-4" aria-label="Link to rule 813.4">813.4.</a> Some passive abilities may grant a card or ability Reaction under certain conditions. The card or ability does not have the Reaction keyword unless and until those circumstances are true.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-813-4-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-813-4-a" aria-label="Link to rule 813.4.a">813.4.a.</a> Those conditions might only be fulfilled while the card or ability is on the chain. In such a case, it can still be played or activated at the appropriate timing as long as doing so could fulfill the conditions.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-813-4-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-813-4-b" aria-label="Link to rule 813.4.b">813.4.b.</a> If the chain item does not fulfill the conditions by the time step 5: check legality has been reached, the actions taken while playing it are undone and it is returned to the zone it was played from if it is a card.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-813-5" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-813-5" aria-label="Link to rule 813.5">813.5.</a> Reaction is a referencable characteristic.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-813-5-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-813-5-a" aria-label="Link to rule 813.5.a">813.5.a.</a> Whether or not a Game Object has Reaction is a characteristic of that Game Object and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-813-5-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-813-5-b" aria-label="Link to rule 813.5.b">813.5.b.</a> Whether or not a Spell has Reaction is a characteristic of that Spell and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-813-5-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-813-5-c" aria-label="Link to rule 813.5.c">813.5.c.</a> Whether or not an Ability has Reaction is a characteristic of that Ability and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-814" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-814" aria-label="Link to rule 814">814.</a> Shield</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-814-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-814-1" aria-label="Link to rule 814.1">814.1.</a> Shield is a Passive Ability keyword.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-814-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-814-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 814.1.a">814.1.a.</a> It is present on Units.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-814-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-814-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 814.1.b">814.1.b.</a> Shield is formatted as "Shield [X]".</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-814-1-b-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-814-1-b-1" aria-label="Link to rule 814.1.b.1">814.1.b.1.</a> The X is referenced in the functional text of the ability.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-814-1-b-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-814-1-b-2" aria-label="Link to rule 814.1.b.2">814.1.b.2.</a> The X is referred to as the Shield Value.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-814-1-b-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-814-1-b-3" aria-label="Link to rule 814.1.b.3">814.1.b.3.</a> If X is omitted, it is presumed to be 1.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-814-1-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-814-1-c" aria-label="Link to rule 814.1.c">814.1.c.</a> It is functionally short for "While I am a defender, I have +X [M]."</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-814-1-d" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-814-1-d" aria-label="Link to rule 814.1.d">814.1.d.</a> Being a defender means the Unit has gained the Defender designation during Combat.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-461">461</a>. Combat for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-814-1-d-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-814-1-d-1" aria-label="Link to rule 814.1.d.1">814.1.d.1.</a> Shield remains in effect as long as the Unit maintains the Defender designation.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-814-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-814-2" aria-label="Link to rule 814.2">814.2.</a> If a Unit has Shield, or has been granted Shield, and is granted Shield by an additional source, the Shield Value of all granted Shield keywords is summed.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: Stalwart Poro has Shield. It is chosen as the target of Block, which says "Give a unit [Shield 3] and [Tank] this turn." After Block resolves, Stalwart Poro has Shield 4 this turn.</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-814-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-814-3" aria-label="Link to rule 814.3">814.3.</a> Shield, and whether or not a unit has Shield, is a characteristic of the Unit and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-815" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-815" aria-label="Link to rule 815">815.</a> Tank</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-815-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-815-1" aria-label="Link to rule 815.1">815.1.</a> Tank is a Passive Ability keyword.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-815-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-815-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 815.1.a">815.1.a.</a> It is present on Units.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-815-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-815-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 815.1.b">815.1.b.</a> It is functionally short for "I must be assigned lethal damage before any other unit with the same controller as me that does not have [Tank] during the Combat Damage step."</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-815-1-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-815-1-c" aria-label="Link to rule 815.1.c">815.1.c.</a> It alters how players can elect to assign combat damage during combat.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-815-1-c-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-815-1-c-1" aria-label="Link to rule 815.1.c.1">815.1.c.1.</a> Players must still assign lethal damage to a unit before moving to the next when assigning their damage.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-815-1-c-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-815-1-c-2" aria-label="Link to rule 815.1.c.2">815.1.c.2.</a> If more than one unit with Tank is present with the same controller in Combat, damage may be assigned to any of them. Units without Tank are invalid assignments until all units with Tank have lethal damage assigned to them.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-815-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-815-2" aria-label="Link to rule 815.2">815.2.</a> Multiple instances of Tank are redundant.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-815-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-815-3" aria-label="Link to rule 815.3">815.3.</a> Tank, and whether or not a unit has Tank, is a characteristic of the Unit and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-816" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-816" aria-label="Link to rule 816">816.</a> Temporary</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-816-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-816-1" aria-label="Link to rule 816.1">816.1.</a> Temporary is a Triggered Ability keyword.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-816-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-816-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 816.1.a">816.1.a.</a> It is present on Permanents.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-816-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-816-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 816.1.b">816.1.b.</a> It is functionally short for "At the start of this permanent's controller's Beginning Phase, before scoring, kill this."</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-816-1-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-816-1-c" aria-label="Link to rule 816.1.c">816.1.c.</a> The Trigger Condition is the controller of the permanent's Beginning Phase starting.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-816-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-816-2" aria-label="Link to rule 816.2">816.2.</a> Multiple instances of Temporary are redundant.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-816-2-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-816-2-a" aria-label="Link to rule 816.2.a">816.2.a.</a> Regardless of how many instances there are, the ability will only trigger once.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-816-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-816-3" aria-label="Link to rule 816.3">816.3.</a> Temporary, and whether or not a permanent has Temporary, is a characteristic of the permanent and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-817" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-817" aria-label="Link to rule 817">817.</a> Vision</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-817-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-817-1" aria-label="Link to rule 817.1">817.1.</a> Vision is a Triggered Ability keyword.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-817-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-817-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 817.1.a">817.1.a.</a> It is present on Permanents.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-817-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-817-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 817.1.b">817.1.b.</a> It is functionally short for "When this is played, [Predict].”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-817-1-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-817-1-c" aria-label="Link to rule 817.1.c">817.1.c.</a> The trigger is the permanent entering the Board.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-817-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-817-2" aria-label="Link to rule 817.2">817.2.</a> Multiple instances of Vision trigger separately.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-817-2-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-817-2-a" aria-label="Link to rule 817.2.a">817.2.a.</a> The player may choose to recycle or not recycle for each instance of Vision separately.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-817-2-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-817-2-b" aria-label="Link to rule 817.2.b">817.2.b.</a> If the player does not recycle the top card and nothing else happens in between the triggers resolving, each instance of Vision will see the same card.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-817-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-817-3" aria-label="Link to rule 817.3">817.3.</a> Vision, and whether or not a permanent has Vision, is a characteristic of the permanent and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-818" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-818" aria-label="Link to rule 818">818.</a> Equip</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-818-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-818-1" aria-label="Link to rule 818.1">818.1.</a> Equip is an Activated Ability keyword.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-818-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-818-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 818.1.a">818.1.a.</a> Equip is present on Gear with the tag Equipment.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-818-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-818-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 818.1.b">818.1.b.</a> Equip has a cost to activate and Attaches the card with Equip to a chosen Unit when the cost is paid.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-818-1-b-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-818-1-b-1" aria-label="Link to rule 818.1.b.1">818.1.b.1.</a> Equip’s choice is a Target.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-818-1-b-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-818-1-b-2" aria-label="Link to rule 818.1.b.2">818.1.b.2.</a> The chosen Unit will become the Top-Most Card for the Attach action.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-818-1-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-818-1-c" aria-label="Link to rule 818.1.c">818.1.c.</a> Equip is formatted as “Equip [Cost]”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-818-1-c-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-818-1-c-1" aria-label="Link to rule 818.1.c.1">818.1.c.1.</a> If paying costs or making choices for this ability causes triggered abilities to trigger, they will be placed on the chain above this ability in a Pending state.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-376">376</a>. Activated Abilities for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-818-1-c-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-818-1-c-2" aria-label="Link to rule 818.1.c.2">818.1.c.2.</a> Equip is functionally short for “[Cost]: Attach this gear to a unit you control.”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-818-1-c-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-818-1-c-3" aria-label="Link to rule 818.1.c.3">818.1.c.3.</a> Equip costs may include both resource costs and non-resource costs.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-818-1-c-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-818-1-c-4" aria-label="Link to rule 818.1.c.4">818.1.c.4.</a> Equip abilities may also include text that alters the Equip cost. Such text is taken into account when determining a card’s Equip cost when paying for the ability.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-818-1-c-5" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-818-1-c-5" aria-label="Link to rule 818.1.c.5">818.1.c.5.</a> Equip abilities may include text that alters the timing or targeting of the Equip ability.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-818-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-818-2" aria-label="Link to rule 818.2">818.2.</a> When the Attach action completes from this keyword, the Unit that was chosen is considered to have been Equipped by the Gear with this ability.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-818-2-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-818-2-a" aria-label="Link to rule 818.2.a">818.2.a.</a> This is an event other Game Effects and Triggered Abilities can reference.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-818-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-818-3" aria-label="Link to rule 818.3">818.3.</a> Equipped is the state of a Top-Most Card being Attached by one or more cards that are Equipment.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-818-3-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-818-3-a" aria-label="Link to rule 818.3.a">818.3.a.</a> The state of being Equipped is synchronous with that of the Attached state of the Equipment.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-818-3-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-818-3-b" aria-label="Link to rule 818.3.b">818.3.b.</a> A Top-Most Card is Equipped as long as one or more of its Attached cards are Equipment.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-818-3-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-818-3-c" aria-label="Link to rule 818.3.c">818.3.c.</a> The state of being Equipped corresponds to a Top-Most card having a card with Equip that is Attached to it.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-818-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-818-4" aria-label="Link to rule 818.4">818.4.</a> Multiple instances of Equip are equivalent to multiple Activated Abilities and can each be activated separately by paying the corresponding costs.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-818-5" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-818-5" aria-label="Link to rule 818.5">818.5.</a> Equip, and whether or not a Gear has Equip, is a characteristic of the Gear and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-818-5-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-818-5-a" aria-label="Link to rule 818.5.a">818.5.a.</a> Whether or not a Gear has Equip may be referenced even if the Rules Text of the Gear is Inactive.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-716">716</a>. Attachment for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-819" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-819" aria-label="Link to rule 819">819.</a> Quick-Draw</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-819-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-819-1" aria-label="Link to rule 819.1">819.1.</a> Quick-Draw is a Triggered Ability keyword. It is also a Permissive keyword.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-819-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-819-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 819.1.a">819.1.a.</a> Quick-Draw is present on Gear with Equip abilities.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-819-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-819-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 819.1.b">819.1.b.</a> Cards with Quick-Draw have Reaction inherently.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-819-1-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-819-1-c" aria-label="Link to rule 819.1.c">819.1.c.</a> Quick-Draw allows cards to be played and Attached using Reaction timing.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-819-1-d" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-819-1-d" aria-label="Link to rule 819.1.d">819.1.d.</a> Quick-Draw is functionally short for “[Reaction]” and “When you play this, attach it to a Unit you control.”</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-716">716</a>. Attachment for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-819-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-819-2" aria-label="Link to rule 819.2">819.2.</a> Multiple instances of Quick-Draw do not trigger separately and have no effect beyond the first.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-819-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-819-3" aria-label="Link to rule 819.3">819.3.</a> Quick-Draw, and whether or not a gear has Quick-Draw, is a characteristic of the Gear and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-820" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-820" aria-label="Link to rule 820">820.</a> Repeat</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-820-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-820-1" aria-label="Link to rule 820.1">820.1.</a> Repeat is an Optional Additional Cost keyword.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-820-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-820-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 820.1.a">820.1.a.</a> Repeat is present on Spells and Abilities.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-820-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-820-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 820.1.b">820.1.b.</a> Repeat is an optional cost that a player may pay to execute the effect of their spells and abilities a second time.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-820-1-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-820-1-c" aria-label="Link to rule 820.1.c">820.1.c.</a> Repeat is formatted as “Repeat [Cost]”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-820-1-c-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-820-1-c-1" aria-label="Link to rule 820.1.c.1">820.1.c.1.</a> The Cost is an Additional Cost to be paid during the steps of playing the spell or ability.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-820-1-c-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-820-1-c-2" aria-label="Link to rule 820.1.c.2">820.1.c.2.</a> If a spell or ability has more than one instance of Repeat, each Cost may be paid or not paid individually.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-820-1-c-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-820-1-c-3" aria-label="Link to rule 820.1.c.3">820.1.c.3.</a> Each Repeat Cost can be paid only a single time.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-820-1-d" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-820-1-d" aria-label="Link to rule 820.1.d">820.1.d.</a> Repeat is functionally short for “You may pay [Cost] as an additional cost as you play this. If you do, execute the instructions of this chain item one additional time during resolution.”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-820-1-d-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-820-1-d-1" aria-label="Link to rule 820.1.d.1">820.1.d.1.</a> When the additional cost is paid, the effect of the spell or ability, upon resolution, will be performed an additional time.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: Desert’s Call is a spell with [Repeat] [2] and “Play a 2 [M] Sand Soldier unit token.” If its controller pays its Repeat cost as they play it, the card’s instruction to play a Sand Soldier is executed twice, as though the card says “Play a 2 [M] Sand Soldier unit token. Play a 2 [M] Sand Soldier unit token.”</p></blockquote>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: A unit reads in part “When I attack or defend, you may deal damage equal to my Might to an enemy unit here,” and “My abilities have [Repeat] — Discard 1.” When its ability triggers, if its controller pays the Repeat cost as they play it, the ability’s instruction to deal damage equal to the unit’s Might is executed twice, as though the ability says “Deal damage equal to my Might to an enemy unit here. Deal damage equal to my Might to an enemy unit here.”</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-820-1-d-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-820-1-d-2" aria-label="Link to rule 820.1.d.2">820.1.d.2.</a> Any instructions not performed on resolution of the spell or ability are ignored.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-820-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-820-2" aria-label="Link to rule 820.2">820.2.</a> When a spell or ability’s effect is performed an additional time with Repeat, choices must be made at the usual time during the Make Relevant Choices step of Playing a Card.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-349">349</a>. Playing Cards for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-820-2-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-820-2-a" aria-label="Link to rule 820.2.a">820.2.a.</a> Choices made for the additional execution do not have to be the same as the choices made for the initial execution.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: Rocket Barrage is a spell with [Repeat] [4][C] and “Choose one — Deal 4 to a unit in a base. [or] Kill a gear.” If Rocket Barrage’s controller pays its Repeat cost as they play it, they may choose the same mode or a different one, and if they choose the same mode, may choose the same target or a different one. If they choose “Kill a gear” twice and choose two different gear, they must specify which gear is the first target and which is the second. As the spell resolves, those two gear will be killed in the chosen order.</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-820-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-820-3" aria-label="Link to rule 820.3">820.3.</a> Multiple instances of Repeat can be paid for separately. The spell or ability’s instructions will be executed an additional time on resolution for each instance of Repeat that is paid for.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-820-3-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-820-3-a" aria-label="Link to rule 820.3.a">820.3.a.</a> Regardless of the number of times a spell or ability's instructions are executed with this keyword, it is only Played once.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-820-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-820-4" aria-label="Link to rule 820.4">820.4.</a> Repeat, and whether or not a spell or ability has Repeat, is a characteristic of the spell or ability and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-821" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-821" aria-label="Link to rule 821">821.</a> Weaponmaster</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-821-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-821-1" aria-label="Link to rule 821.1">821.1.</a> Weaponmaster is a Triggered Ability keyword.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-821-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-821-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 821.1.a">821.1.a.</a> Weaponmaster is present on Units.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-821-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-821-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 821.1.b">821.1.b.</a> Weaponmaster is a Play Effect that chooses an Equipment you control and allows you to pay its Equip cost at a discount, regardless of the usual timing of the Equip ability, to Attach that Equipment to the unit with Weaponmaster.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-821-1-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-821-1-c" aria-label="Link to rule 821.1.c">821.1.c.</a> Weaponmaster is functionally short for: “When you play me, you may choose a Card you control with the Equipment tag. Necessary portions of its Rules Text are no longer Inactive if they are currently Inactive. Pay the cost of its Equip ability, reduced by [A], to attach it to this unit.”</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-716">716</a>. Attachment for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-821-1-c-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-821-1-c-1" aria-label="Link to rule 821.1.c.1">821.1.c.1.</a> Weaponmaster can choose an Equipment whether it has an Equip ability or not.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-821-1-c-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-821-1-c-2" aria-label="Link to rule 821.1.c.2">821.1.c.2.</a> The cost of the Equip ability is determined as though that Equip ability was being activated choosing the unit with the Weaponmaster ability, as modulated by any abilities that alter Equip costs.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-821-1-c-2-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 4">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-821-1-c-2-a" aria-label="Link to rule 821.1.c.2.a">821.1.c.2.a.</a> The Equip ability is not actually activated when its cost is determined in this way. An effect that grants an activated ability a cost reduction when it is played will not apply to the Equip cost calculated during the resolution of a Weaponmaster trigger, because the Equip ability is not actually played.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-821-1-c-2-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 4">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-821-1-c-2-b" aria-label="Link to rule 821.1.c.2.b">821.1.c.2.b.</a> Effects that reduce the cost of Equip abilities without referring to those abilities being played, or the Equip ability being referenced in specific, will be included when calculating costs in this way.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-821-1-c-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-821-1-c-3" aria-label="Link to rule 821.1.c.3">821.1.c.3.</a> If the chosen card’s Equip cost does not contain [A], it can still be paid, but will not be reduced.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-821-1-c-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-821-1-c-4" aria-label="Link to rule 821.1.c.4">821.1.c.4.</a> If the chosen card doesn’t have an Equip cost, it can’t be paid.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-821-1-c-5" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-821-1-c-5" aria-label="Link to rule 821.1.c.5">821.1.c.5.</a> If the chosen card’s Equip cost can’t be paid, if it can’t be detached from its current Top-Most card, or if it can’t be attached to the unit with the Weaponmaster ability, it stays in its current location, Attached to anything it was already Attached to.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-821-1-c-6" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-821-1-c-6" aria-label="Link to rule 821.1.c.6">821.1.c.6.</a> The Equip ability is not activated this way, and the unit with the Weaponmaster ability is not chosen.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-821-1-c-7" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-821-1-c-7" aria-label="Link to rule 821.1.c.7">821.1.c.7.</a> Multiple instances of Weaponmaster trigger separately, and can choose different targets.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-821-1-d" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-821-1-d" aria-label="Link to rule 821.1.d">821.1.d.</a> If you choose the same target with multiple instances of Weaponmaster, each will resolve separately.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-821-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-821-2" aria-label="Link to rule 821.2">821.2.</a> Weaponmaster has no function while on the board.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-821-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-821-3" aria-label="Link to rule 821.3">821.3.</a> Weaponmaster, and whether or not a unit has Weaponmaster, is a characteristic of the Unit and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-822" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-822" aria-label="Link to rule 822">822.</a> Ambush</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-822-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-822-1" aria-label="Link to rule 822.1">822.1.</a> Ambush is a Passive Ability keyword.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-822-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-822-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 822.1.a">822.1.a.</a> It is normally present on Units.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-822-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-822-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 822.1.b">822.1.b.</a> It is functionally short for "I may be played to a battlefield where you control Units" and “I have [Reaction] as long as I’m being played to a battlefield where you control Units.”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-822-1-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-822-1-c" aria-label="Link to rule 822.1.c">822.1.c.</a> It is a passive ability that adds options to locations that are valid for a Unit to be played to during the Make Relevant Choices step of Playing a Card</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-349">349</a>. Playing Cards for more information</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-822-1-d" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-822-1-d" aria-label="Link to rule 822.1.d">822.1.d.</a> Ambush can also appear as a verb on a card. In such a case, the verb is taken to mean “play with the permissions of the Ambush keyword.”</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: Rengar, Trophy Hunter reads in part “I can [Ambush] to a battlefield where there are enemy units, even if you don't have units there.” This ability expands the normal permissions of the Ambush keyword to include battlefields where there are enemy units.</p></blockquote>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: A card reads in part “Opponents can only [Ambush] at any time they could play cards with [Action].” This ability introduces a restriction to when opponents can play cards with Ambush.</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-822-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-822-2" aria-label="Link to rule 822.2">822.2.</a> Multiple instances of Ambush are redundant.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-822-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-822-3" aria-label="Link to rule 822.3">822.3.</a> If there are no units at the location chosen before Finalization completes for any reason, then it is no longer a valid location by Ambush’s reasoning and cannot be played there</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-822-3-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-822-3-a" aria-label="Link to rule 822.3.a">822.3.a.</a> Other effects and permissions may still enable this Unit to be able to be played to the selected location, but Ambush’s permission will not be valid</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-822-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-822-4" aria-label="Link to rule 822.4">822.4.</a> Ambush, and whether or not a unit has Ambush, is a characteristic of the Unit and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-823" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-823" aria-label="Link to rule 823">823.</a> Hunt</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-823-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-823-1" aria-label="Link to rule 823.1">823.1.</a> Hunt is a Triggered Ability keyword.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-823-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-823-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 823.1.a">823.1.a.</a> Hunt is present on Units.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-823-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-823-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 823.1.b">823.1.b.</a> Hunt is both a Conquer and a Hold effect.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-823-1-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-823-1-c" aria-label="Link to rule 823.1.c">823.1.c.</a> Hunt is formatted as “Hunt X”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-823-1-c-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-823-1-c-1" aria-label="Link to rule 823.1.c.1">823.1.c.1.</a> Hunt is functionally short for: “When I Conquer or Hold, my controller gains X XP.”</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-728">728</a>. XP for more information</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-823-1-c-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-823-1-c-2" aria-label="Link to rule 823.1.c.2">823.1.c.2.</a> If X is omitted, it is presumed to be 1.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-823-1-c-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-823-1-c-3" aria-label="Link to rule 823.1.c.3">823.1.c.3.</a> X is referred to as the Hunt Value.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-823-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-823-2" aria-label="Link to rule 823.2">823.2.</a> If a Unit has Hunt, or has been granted Hunt, and is granted Hunt by an additional source, the Hunt Value of all granted Hunt keywords is summed.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-823-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-823-3" aria-label="Link to rule 823.3">823.3.</a> Hunt, and whether or not a unit has Hunt, is a characteristic of the Unit and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-824" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-824" aria-label="Link to rule 824">824.</a> Level</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-824-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-824-1" aria-label="Link to rule 824.1">824.1.</a> Level is a Dependent Keyword.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-824-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-824-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 824.1.a">824.1.a.</a> It is formatted as "[Level [N][&gt;] [Text]".</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-824-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-824-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 824.1.b">824.1.b.</a> Starting from the Keyword to the end of the clause, the entire statement is the Level Ability.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-824-1-b-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-824-1-b-1" aria-label="Link to rule 824.1.b.1">824.1.b.1.</a> It is functionally short for “While you have [N] or more XP, this card gains ‘[Text]’.”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-824-1-b-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-824-1-b-2" aria-label="Link to rule 824.1.b.2">824.1.b.2.</a> The [Text] here is the Dependent Ability</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-824-1-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-824-1-c" aria-label="Link to rule 824.1.c">824.1.c.</a> As long as the controlling player has [N] XP, then the Dependent Ability will be be Active on the card with Level</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-824-1-c-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-824-1-c-1" aria-label="Link to rule 824.1.c.1">824.1.c.1.</a> If the controller of the card with Level changes, then the Dependent Ability will be rendered Active or Inactive based on the new controller’s XP.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-824-1-d" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-824-1-d" aria-label="Link to rule 824.1.d">824.1.d.</a> The Dependent Ability will be Inactive as soon as the controlling player has less than [N] XP.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-824-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-824-2" aria-label="Link to rule 824.2">824.2.</a> Level, and whether or not a card has Level is a characteristic of the card and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-825" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-825" aria-label="Link to rule 825">825.</a> Unique</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-825-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-825-1" aria-label="Link to rule 825.1">825.1.</a> Unique is a Deck Constraint Permission.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-825-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-825-2" aria-label="Link to rule 825.2">825.2.</a> It is normally present on main deck cards.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-825-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-825-3" aria-label="Link to rule 825.3">825.3.</a> Unique is not functionally short for any rules text, and instead provides a restriction to players during Deck Construction.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-825-3-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-825-3-a" aria-label="Link to rule 825.3.a">825.3.a.</a> A deck can contain only one card of a given name if the card has Unique</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-825-3-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-825-3-b" aria-label="Link to rule 825.3.b">825.3.b.</a> If a card is a Signature card and is also Unique, then that deck can contain any combination of three Signature cards, but still only one of each named Unique card.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-825-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-825-4" aria-label="Link to rule 825.4">825.4.</a> Cards with Unique have no additional effects during gameplay.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-825-5" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-825-5" aria-label="Link to rule 825.5">825.5.</a> Unique, and whether or not a Card has Unique, is a characteristic of the Unit and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-826" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-826" aria-label="Link to rule 826">826.</a> Backline</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-826-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-826-1" aria-label="Link to rule 826.1">826.1.</a> Backline is a Passive Ability keyword.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-826-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-826-2" aria-label="Link to rule 826.2">826.2.</a> It is present on Units.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-826-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-826-3" aria-label="Link to rule 826.3">826.3.</a> It is functionally short for “I must be assigned lethal damage after any other unit with the same controller as me that does not have [Backline] during the Combat Damage step.”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-826-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-826-4" aria-label="Link to rule 826.4">826.4.</a> It alters how players can elect to assign combat damage during combat.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-826-4-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-826-4-a" aria-label="Link to rule 826.4.a">826.4.a.</a> Players must still assign lethal damage to a unit before moving to the next when assigning their damage.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-826-4-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-826-4-b" aria-label="Link to rule 826.4.b">826.4.b.</a> If more than one unit with Backline is present with the same controller in Combat, damage may be assigned to any of them. Units with Backline are invalid assignments until all units without Backline have lethal damage assigned to them.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-826-5" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-826-5" aria-label="Link to rule 826.5">826.5.</a> Multiple Instances of Backline are redundant.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-826-6" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-826-6" aria-label="Link to rule 826.6">826.6.</a> Backline, and whether or not a unit has Backline, is a characteristic of the Unit and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-827" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-827" aria-label="Link to rule 827">827.</a> Empower</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-827-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-827-1" aria-label="Link to rule 827.1">827.1.</a> Empower is an Activated Ability keyword.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-827-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-827-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 827.1.a">827.1.a.</a> Empower is normally present on permanents and legends.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-827-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-827-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 827.1.b">827.1.b.</a> Empower has a cost to activate and Empowers the source of the ability when the cost is paid.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-442">442</a>. Empower (Game Action) for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-827-1-b-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-827-1-b-1" aria-label="Link to rule 827.1.b.1">827.1.b.1.</a> The source game object is not a target of the Empower ability.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-827-1-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-827-1-c" aria-label="Link to rule 827.1.c">827.1.c.</a> Empower is formatted as “Empower [Cost]”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-827-1-c-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-827-1-c-1" aria-label="Link to rule 827.1.c.1">827.1.c.1.</a> Empower is functionally short for “[Cost]: Empower this. Play only if not Empowered.”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-827-1-c-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-827-1-c-2" aria-label="Link to rule 827.1.c.2">827.1.c.2.</a> Empower costs may include both resource costs and non-resource costs.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-827-1-c-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-827-1-c-3" aria-label="Link to rule 827.1.c.3">827.1.c.3.</a> Empower abilities may also include text that alters the Empower cost. Such text is taken into account when determining a card’s Empower cost for any reason.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-827-1-c-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-827-1-c-4" aria-label="Link to rule 827.1.c.4">827.1.c.4.</a> Empower abilities may include text that alters the timing of the Empower ability.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-827-1-c-5" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-827-1-c-5" aria-label="Link to rule 827.1.c.5">827.1.c.5.</a> Game Objects with Empower abilities may have or gain permissions to be Empowered multiple times. Such Game Objects ignore the normal “Play only if not Empowered.” restriction on Empower abilities.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-827-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-827-2" aria-label="Link to rule 827.2">827.2.</a> When the Empower action completes from this keyword, the source permanent or legend becomes Empowered.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-827-2-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-827-2-a" aria-label="Link to rule 827.2.a">827.2.a.</a> This is an event other Game Effects and Triggered Abilities can reference.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-827-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-827-3" aria-label="Link to rule 827.3">827.3.</a> Multiple instances of Empower are equivalent to multiple activated abilities and can each be activated separately by paying the corresponding costs.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-827-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-827-4" aria-label="Link to rule 827.4">827.4.</a> Empower and whether a permanent or legend has Empower are a characteristic of those Game Objects and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-828" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-828" aria-label="Link to rule 828">828.</a> Empowered</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-828-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-828-1" aria-label="Link to rule 828.1">828.1.</a> Empowered is a Dependent Keyword.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-828-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-828-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 828.1.a">828.1.a.</a> It is formatted as “[Empowered][&gt;] [Text].”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-828-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-828-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 828.1.b">828.1.b.</a> Starting from the keyword to the end of the clause, the entire statement is the Empowered Ability.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-828-1-b-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-828-1-b-1" aria-label="Link to rule 828.1.b.1">828.1.b.1.</a> It is functionally short for “While I have the Empowered status, this card gains ‘[Text]’.”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-828-1-b-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-828-1-b-2" aria-label="Link to rule 828.1.b.2">828.1.b.2.</a> The [Text] here is the Dependent Ability.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-828-1-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-828-1-c" aria-label="Link to rule 828.1.c">828.1.c.</a> As long as the Game Object has the Empowered status, then the Dependent Ability will be active.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-828-1-d" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-828-1-d" aria-label="Link to rule 828.1.d">828.1.d.</a> If the Dependent Ability is a Triggered Ability whose condition is “When I become Empowered,” or a permutation thereof, it will be active and trigger when its source becomes Empowered.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-828-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-828-2" aria-label="Link to rule 828.2">828.2.</a> Empowered and whether or not a card has an Empowered Ability is a characteristic of the card that may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-829" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-829" aria-label="Link to rule 829">829.</a> Flow</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-829-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-829-1" aria-label="Link to rule 829.1">829.1.</a> Flow is a Passive Ability keyword.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-829-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-829-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 829.1.a">829.1.a.</a> Flow is present on Spells.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-829-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-829-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 829.1.b">829.1.b.</a> It is functionally short for “You may play this from your trash for its flow cost. Then banish it.”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-829-1-b-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-829-1-b-1" aria-label="Link to rule 829.1.b.1">829.1.b.1.</a> Banishing the spell in this way is a delayed replacement effect. If the spell would leave the chain after becoming a finalized chain item, and leaving the chain wasn’t instructed by its own execution, banish it instead.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-367">367</a>. Replacement Effects for more information.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-390">390</a>. Delayed Abilities for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-829-1-b-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-829-1-b-2" aria-label="Link to rule 829.1.b.2">829.1.b.2.</a> Playing a spell for its Flow cost does not change the timing at which it can be played, nor any permissions for the spell aside from the zone from which it can be played.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-829-1-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-829-1-c" aria-label="Link to rule 829.1.c">829.1.c.</a> Flow is formatted as “Flow [Cost]”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-829-1-c-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-829-1-c-1" aria-label="Link to rule 829.1.c.1">829.1.c.1.</a> The cost is an alternate cost that replaces the base cost of the spell to be paid during finalization.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-829-1-c-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-829-1-c-2" aria-label="Link to rule 829.1.c.2">829.1.c.2.</a> Flow costs may include both resource costs and non-resource costs.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-829-1-c-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-829-1-c-3" aria-label="Link to rule 829.1.c.3">829.1.c.3.</a> If a spell has multiple instances of the Flow keyword with different costs, its controller may choose which cost to apply as they play it.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-829-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-829-2" aria-label="Link to rule 829.2">829.2.</a> Flow, and whether or not a spell has Flow, is a characteristic of the Spell and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-830" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-830" aria-label="Link to rule 830">830.</a> Deploy</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-830-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-830-1" aria-label="Link to rule 830.1">830.1.</a> Deploy is a Passive Ability and a Triggered Ability keyword.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-830-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-830-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 830.1.a">830.1.a.</a> Deploy is present on Gear.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-830-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-830-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 830.1.b">830.1.b.</a> Deploy is short for “This may be played or created to any battlefield,” “Play or create this only to a battlefield,” “If this is not a unit, it is not recalled in cleanups,” and “When an opponent holds here, kill this.”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-830-1-b-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-830-1-b-1" aria-label="Link to rule 830.1.b.1">830.1.b.1.</a> Deploy grants permission for Gear to be played to any Battlefield, including enemy occupied and open battlefields.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-830-1-b-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-830-1-b-2" aria-label="Link to rule 830.1.b.2">830.1.b.2.</a> Gear with Deploy can be Moved by appropriate effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-830-1-b-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-830-1-b-3" aria-label="Link to rule 830.1.b.3">830.1.b.3.</a> Non-Unit Gear with Deploy are not recalled from Battlefields in step 5 of a Cleanup.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-318">318</a>. Cleanups for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-830-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-830-2" aria-label="Link to rule 830.2">830.2.</a> Multiple instances of Deploy are redundant.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-830-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-830-3" aria-label="Link to rule 830.3">830.3.</a> Deploy, and whether or not a card has Deploy, is a characteristic of the card and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-831" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-831" aria-label="Link to rule 831">831.</a> Disarm</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-831-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-831-1" aria-label="Link to rule 831.1">831.1.</a> Disarm is a Triggered Ability keyword.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-831-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-831-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 831.1.a">831.1.a.</a> Disarm is present on Units.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-831-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-831-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 831.1.b">831.1.b.</a> Disarm is an Attack Trigger.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-831-1-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-831-1-c" aria-label="Link to rule 831.1.c">831.1.c.</a> Disarm is formatted as “Disarm X.”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-831-1-c-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-831-1-c-1" aria-label="Link to rule 831.1.c.1">831.1.c.1.</a> The X is referenced in the functional text of the ability.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-831-1-c-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-831-1-c-2" aria-label="Link to rule 831.1.c.2">831.1.c.2.</a> The X is referred to as the Disarm Value.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-831-1-c-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-831-1-c-3" aria-label="Link to rule 831.1.c.3">831.1.c.3.</a> If X is omitted, it is presumed to be 1.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-831-1-d" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-831-1-d" aria-label="Link to rule 831.1.d">831.1.d.</a> Disarm is functionally short for “When I attack, give an enemy unit here -X [M] this turn.”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-831-1-d-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-831-1-d-1" aria-label="Link to rule 831.1.d.1">831.1.d.1.</a> Regardless of how many instances of the Disarm keyword a unit has, it will only trigger once using the total Disarm Value.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-831-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-831-2" aria-label="Link to rule 831.2">831.2.</a> If a Unit has Disarm, or has been granted Disarm, and is granted Disarm by an additional source, the Disarm Value of all granted Disarm keywords is summed.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: Clanging Sentry reads “Your Mechs have [Disarm].” If you play a second Clanging Sentry, both of them will have Disarm 2.</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-831-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-831-3" aria-label="Link to rule 831.3">831.3.</a> Disarm, and whether or not a unit has Disarm, is a characteristic of the Unit and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-832" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 0">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-832" aria-label="Link to rule 832">832.</a> Show Off</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-832-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-832-1" aria-label="Link to rule 832.1">832.1.</a> Show Off is a Passive Ability keyword.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-832-1-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-832-1-a" aria-label="Link to rule 832.1.a">832.1.a.</a> Show Off is normally present on Cards.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-832-1-b" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-832-1-b" aria-label="Link to rule 832.1.b">832.1.b.</a> Show Off is formatted as “[Show Off] [Object].”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-832-1-b-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-832-1-b-1" aria-label="Link to rule 832.1.b.1">832.1.b.1.</a> The “[Object]” in this ability could be one object or multiple; object/s of a given type; or it could have further specifications on qualities of the object/s. The object in question could be Game Objects on the board or cards in hand.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Examples: A card reads “[Show Off] a unit.” A card reads “[Show Off] a card with Energy cost [7] or more.” A card reads “[Show Off] five units with different names.”</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-832-1-c" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-832-1-c" aria-label="Link to rule 832.1.c">832.1.c.</a> Show Off is functionally short for “As you play [me/this], you may reveal [Object] from your hand and/or pick [Object] from among friendly game objects on the board.”</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-832-1-c-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-832-1-c-1" aria-label="Link to rule 832.1.c.1">832.1.c.1.</a> Showing Off one or more Cards or Game Objects in this way can be referenced by linked abilities and instructions.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: A unit reads in part “[Show Off] five units with different names,” and “When you play me, kill all other units. If you showed off, the next unit you play this turn costs [1] less for each killed unit.”</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-832-1-c-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-832-1-c-2" aria-label="Link to rule 832.1.c.2">832.1.c.2.</a> The decision of whether or not to Show Off is made by the controller of the chain item with Show Off during the process of finalizing it.</p>
<p class="rule-reference">See rule <a href="#rule-355">355</a>. Make Relevant Choices for more information.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-832-1-c-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-832-1-c-3" aria-label="Link to rule 832.1.c.3">832.1.c.3.</a> If [Object] is a set of Cards or Game Objects, that set can be composed of Game Objects on the board as well as Cards in hand, as long as the composed set satisfies the Show Off condition.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: A unit reads in part “[Show Off] five units with different names.” When showing off as part of playing that unit, units may be revealed from hand or picked from among friendly units on the board, as long as the group of units picked and revealed satisfies the specified quality.</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-832-1-c-4" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-832-1-c-4" aria-label="Link to rule 832.1.c.4">832.1.c.4.</a> If some or all of the [Object] set leave the appropriate zones during the finalization process after being shown off, it will not prevent the source of the Show Off ability from becoming a Finalized Chain Item.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: A spell reads “[Show Off] a Mech” and “As an additional cost to play this, discard 1.” When finalizing the spell, the same Mech being shown off can be discarded to the mandatory additional cost without preventing the spell from being finalized.</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-832-1-d" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-832-1-d" aria-label="Link to rule 832.1.d">832.1.d.</a> Linked abilities and instructions can reference information about the shown off object/s.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: Primordial Roar is a spell that reads “[Show Off] a unit,” and “If you showed off a unit, deal damage equal to that unit's Might to a unit.”</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-832-1-d-1" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 3">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-832-1-d-1" aria-label="Link to rule 832.1.d.1">832.1.d.1.</a> The information referenced in this way is checked when the card/s in question are shown off during finalization. Any later changes to the shown off objects will not be considered.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: Primordial Roar is played showing off a friendly unit with 4 [M] and an enemy unit with 3 [M]. In reaction to Primordial Roar, an opponent plays Frigid Touch, giving the friendly unit -2 [M] this turn. Because the information was checked when the friendly unit was shown off, the spell still deals 4 damage to the enemy unit, killing it.</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-832-2" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-832-2" aria-label="Link to rule 832.2">832.2.</a> Multiple instances of Show Off are treated separately.</p>
</div>

<div id="rule-832-2-a" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 2">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-832-2-a" aria-label="Link to rule 832.2.a">832.2.a.</a> If a linked effect or instruction refers to an instance of Show Off, it refers to the keyword granted by the same source unless specified otherwise or there is no keyword granted by the same source. If the linked effect or instruction specifies information about the shown off card, it is linked to only that instance of the Show Off keyword. If there is no linked keyword, an ability that references showing off is linked to all Show Off keywords on the appropriate card.</p>
<blockquote class="rule-example"><p>Example: A card reads in part “Spells you play have ‘[Show Off] a Mighty unit.’ and ‘If you showed off, this costs [A] less.” If a spell that reads in part “[Show Off] a spell with Energy cost [7] or more,” and “If you showed off, this can’t be countered.” is played by the same controller, showing off a Mighty unit will not be sufficient to satisfy both abilities that reference showing off because they refer to instances of the show off keyword granted by different sources.</p></blockquote>
</div>

<div id="rule-832-3" class="rule-entry" style="--rule-depth: 1">
<p><a class="rule-number" href="#rule-832-3" aria-label="Link to rule 832.3">832.3.</a> Show Off, and whether or not a card has Show Off, is a characteristic of the card and may be checked or referenced by other Game Effects.</p>
</div>
